# SkyCraft item conversion: which Skyrim items become Minecraft items when you get them.
# One rule per line, first match wins. Anything that matches no rule stays in Skyrim.
#   conditions = minecraft:item N     (N Minecraft items per Skyrim item)
#   conditions = keep                 (stays in Skyrim)
#   conditions = minecraft:item N + minecraft:other M   (several items for one)
#   conditions = minecraft:potion[healing] N   (a vanilla potion; see "Potions and poisons" below)
#   conditions = minecraft:potion{speed:0-1:60-180 + jump_boost:0:60 #3a9a3a "Name"} N
#                                     (a potion with effects of its own; see "Potions and poisons")
#   conditions = skycraft:ebony_sword{fire_aspect:2 + looting:1 "Name"} N
#                                     (gear that keeps a Skyrim enchantment, levels 1 to 10, and
#                                     maybe its name; an enchanted_book stores them)
#   conditions = minecraft:leather_chestplate{#3b5dc9 "Name"} N
#                                     (leather armor dyed a color, for clothing)
# Conditions (all must hold): tag <t>, name <word>, material <m>, from <where>, kind <k>
#   tags: food foodraw hide oreingot potion poison ingredient weapon armor clothing jewelry
#         gold key arrow gem clutter book soulgem filled (a soul gem that comes with a soul)
#   materials: wood iron steel imperial orcish dwarven elven glass ebony daedric dragon
#              silver hide draugr falmer
#   from: world corpse container actor bought (Skyrim's shop) looted (corpse or container); several
#         from conditions mean any of them (from looted from bought)
#   kinds (the piece Skyrim says it is): sword dagger war_axe mace greatsword battleaxe bow staff
#         crossbow hand_to_hand helmet cuirass gauntlets boots shield
# Skyrim sends names cut to 27 letters. SkyCraft gives back the whole name of the long Skyrim
# potion names it knows (Potion of Fortify Two-handed, Potion of Prolonged Invisibility, ...),
# so a name word can be the whole word; the cut word ("hande") matches too.
# Restart Skyrim after editing.

# Stays in Skyrim: coins for merchants, keys, milk, two rare treasures (Sheogorath-Shaped Amber,
# the Exquisite Sapphire). Lockpicks and soul gems you fill yourself stay too, since no rule takes
# them. Books and scrolls, and drinks, have their own rules below.
tag gold = keep
tag key = keep
tag food name milk = keep
name sheogorath = keep
name exquisite = keep

# Books and scrolls. A treasure map is Minecraft's buried treasure map. Every character starts
# knowing Flames and Healing, so those two spell tomes are plain books (the keep lines guard the
# tomes whose names hold those words); every other tome stays. A scroll is the potion of its spell:
# fire, frost and shock bolts and storms splash Instant Damage (frost slows, shock weakens), the
# runes are TNT, the flesh spells Resistance, Courage and Rally Strength and Health Boost, the
# Calm and Fear spells Weakness and Darkness, Frenzy Nausea, healing Instant Health, and turning
# the undead a splash of Instant Health, which harms them. Other books stay.
name treasure name map = minecraft:buried_treasure_map 1
name spell name tome name wall name flames = keep
name spell name tome name healing name hands = keep
name spell name tome name fast name healing = keep
name spell name tome name grand name healing = keep
name spell name tome name flames = minecraft:book 1
name spell name tome name healing = minecraft:book 1
name scroll name firebolt = minecraft:splash_potion{instant_damage:0:0 #e8641e} 1
name scroll name fireball = minecraft:splash_potion{instant_damage:1:0 #e8641e} 1
name scroll name fire name storm = minecraft:lingering_potion{instant_damage:1:0 #e8641e} 1
name scroll name ice name spike = minecraft:splash_potion{instant_damage:0:0 + slowness:1:8 #8fd3ff} 1
name scroll name ice name storm = minecraft:lingering_potion{instant_damage:0:0 + slowness:1:10 #8fd3ff} 1
name scroll name blizzard = minecraft:lingering_potion{instant_damage:1:0 + slowness:2:15 #8fd3ff} 1
name scroll name lightning name bolt = minecraft:splash_potion{instant_damage:0:0 + weakness:0:15 #c9b8ff} 1
name scroll name chain name lightning = minecraft:splash_potion{instant_damage:1:0 + weakness:0:20 #c9b8ff} 1

# Skooma is SkyCraft's Skooma: a high of speed, higher jumps and quick, quiet sneaking, and an addiction
# that comes with it (skycraft-skooma.txt). Redwater Skooma is the stronger cut. Moon sugar's rush is
# also a little stamina (saturation).
name empty name skooma = minecraft:glass_bottle 1
name redwater name skooma = minecraft:potion{skycraft:skooma:1:150 + saturation:1:0 #8e3fb5} 1
name skooma = minecraft:potion{skycraft:skooma:0:180 + saturation:0:0 #b26be0} 1

# Enchanted weapons and armor keep their enchantment, from anywhere: the gear of their material's
# tier (Imperial and Ancient Nord are steel, Falmer orcish, silver iron, dragon daedric, hide and
# leather Minecraft leather, Nordic orcish, Stalhrim ebony) with the nearest Minecraft enchantment,
# at a level that follows the Skyrim name's strength (the lowest third of its ladder I, the middle
# II, the top III or Minecraft's own top). On swords: Fire Damage is Fire Aspect, Frost, Fear and
# Paralysis Knockback, Shock Sharpness, Absorb Health Sharpness (and Mending at the top), Absorb
# Magicka or Stamina Mending, Magicka and Stamina Damage Sweeping Edge, Soul Trap Looting, Turn
# Undead and silver Smite, Huntsman's Prowess Bane of Arthropods. War axes take the axe's
# enchantments: Sharpness for the elements, Unbreaking for Magicka and Stamina Damage, Fortune for
# Soul Trap. Bows: Fire is Flame, Frost, Fear and Paralysis Punch, Shock, Absorb Health and Turn
# Undead Power, Soul Trap Infinity at the top; crossbows Piercing and Quick Charge. Armor: Fortify
# Health, Resist Magic, Frost, Poison and Disease, Heavy and Light Armor are Protection, Resist Fire
# Fire Protection, Resist Shock Blast Protection, Archery Projectile Protection, the weapon skills on
# gauntlets Thorns, Magicka and the magic schools Mending, Sneak and Muffle on boots Feather Falling,
# Waterbreathing Respiration and Aqua Affinity, Carry Weight, Stamina and the crafting and thief
# skills Unbreaking. Shields keep Unbreaking for Blocking and are plain shields otherwise. Skyrim
# sends 27 letters of a name, so the enchantment word is often cut ("of the Infer"), and items
# whose names share their first 27 letters share a rule.

# Potions and poisons
# A Skyrim potion becomes a Minecraft potion with the nearest effects, named like the Skyrim one,
# and a poison becomes a splash potion to throw. Braces list the effects:
#   minecraft:potion{effect:amplifier:seconds + effect:amplifier:seconds #rrggbb "Name"} N
# The amplifier is 0 for level I; seconds is 0 for instant effects. A range like 0-2 grows
# with the Skyrim name's tier: Minor, Weak and Brief are tier 0, a name with none of these
# words tier 1, Plentiful, Draught, Potent, Extended, Enduring and Lasting tier 2, Vigorous,
# Solution, Philter, Virulent, Malign, Persisting and Prolonged tier 3, Extreme, Elixir, Deadly
# and Unceasing tier 4, Ultimate, Grand and Prime tier 5 (words the rule names don't count, so
# "Potion of Lasting Potency" is tier 1). Without "Name" the potion takes the Skyrim name; the
# color is optional (Minecraft mixes the effects' colors).
#   effects: speed slowness haste mining_fatigue strength instant_health instant_damage
#            jump_boost nausea regeneration resistance fire_resistance water_breathing
#            invisibility blindness night_vision hunger weakness poison wither health_boost
#            absorption saturation glowing levitation luck unluck slow_falling conduit_power
#            dolphins_grace bad_omen hero_of_the_village darkness trial_omen raid_omen
#            wind_charged weaving oozing infested breath_of_the_nautilus
# A bracket names a vanilla potion instead; potion, splash_potion, lingering_potion and
# tipped_arrow all take either (minecraft:tipped_arrow[poison] 4 gives four poison arrows).
#   potions: healing strong_healing regeneration long_regeneration strong_regeneration strength
#            long_strength strong_strength swiftness long_swiftness strong_swiftness invisibility
#            long_invisibility night_vision long_night_vision fire_resistance long_fire_resistance
#            water_breathing long_water_breathing leaping long_leaping strong_leaping slow_falling
#            long_slow_falling turtle_master long_turtle_master strong_turtle_master luck poison
#            long_poison strong_poison harming strong_harming weakness long_weakness slowness
#            long_slowness strong_slowness wind_charged weaving oozing infested water awkward
#            mundane thick
# Health potions are red, magicka blue, stamina green. Magicka has no Minecraft meter, so it
# becomes Absorption (a ward of golden hearts); stamina is the hunger bar and running.
# Stay in Skyrim: the White Phial (it refills), the Lotus and Nightshade Extracts (quest poisons),
# Unknown Potion, and drinks without a vendor keyword (Potion of Blood). Skooma has its own rules at the top.
tag potion name phial = keep
name philter name phantom = minecraft:potion{invisibility:0:60} 1
tag potion name night name eye = minecraft:potion{night_vision:0:120-480} 1
tag potion name detect name life = minecraft:potion{night_vision:0:60-240} 1
tag potion name muffle = minecraft:potion{skycraft:stealth:0:30-120 #4d5b66} 1

# a SkyCraft ingot take the nearest: Imperial and Ancient Nord (draugr) gear is steel, Falmer gear is
# chaurus chitin (turtle scute), silver melts to iron nuggets, dragonbone and dragonscale give ebony
# and bone. Every Daedric piece is forged with a Daedra heart and gives it back. Nordic and Forsworn
# weapons are steel, Stalhrim blue ice, Amber resin, Madness crying obsidian, Dark obsidian, Golden gold.

# Clothing, looted or bought, is Minecraft leather armor dyed like it, by body slot: hoods, hats
# and circlets helmets, robes, clothes and tunics chestplates, gloves and bracers leggings (Minecraft
# has no glove slot), boots and shoes boots. Mage robes keep their magic as Mending (they
# regenerate magicka or ease a school's spells). Clothing you make, enchant or pick up stays, and
# so does enchanted clothing no rule names, rather than lose its enchantment. Hearthfire's
# children's clothes carry no clothing tag, so they have rules of their own. A Wedding Wreath is
# its flowers; nothing in Minecraft fits the Moth Priest's blindfold.

# Jewelry, looted or bought: a plain ring or necklace melts into its metal (gold into gold
# nuggets, silver into iron nuggets) and gives its gem (rubies and garnets are redstone, sapphires
# lapis). A circlet is worn on the head, so it's a leather helmet dyed its metal's color, and its
# gem. Enchanted and unique jewelry (Amulets of the Divines, the Daedric rings, Savos Aren's Amulet,
# ...) and jewelry you make or enchant stay in Skyrim.
